observed.
. Drive one required enable low (`ap++_++debug`, then `soc++_++debug`)
and attempt a SMC fabric read.
. Monitor AXI request channels for a bounded window and verify no AW/AR
valid activity occurs.
. Restore the required enable and verify a normal read again produces
AXI activity and OKAY status.

*Checkers*:

[width="100%",cols="52%,48%",options="header",]
|===
|Checker Type |Description
|`smc++_++rd++_++gate++_++no++_++aw++_++ar++_++chk` |No AWVALID or
ARVALID while the bridge is gated

|`smc++_++rd++_++gate++_++restore++_++activity++_++chk` |AXI read
activity resumes after gating clears
|===

*Pass Criteria*: Each required enable independently blocks all SMC
fabric AXI read activity while low and normal reads resume after
restore, with zero scoreboard/reference-model errors.

'''''

===== 3.7 `dtp++_++jtag2axi++_++smc++_++otp++_++axi++_++single++_++write++_++read++_++test`

*Source Module*:
`dtp++_++jtag2axi++_++smc++_++otp++_++axi++_++rd++_++test`

*BFM / Model*: `ocah++_++jtag++_++vip`, `ocah++_++axi++_++vip`

*Objective*: SMC OTP write-read path

*Test Coverage*:

* SMC OTP AXI4-Lite single-operation write followed by a read-back of
the same address
* SMC++_++OTP++_++AXI++_++SINGLE++_++OP (IR=0x1C) DR field packing:
32-bit address, 32-bit data, wstrb, op
* CAPS reporting bus++_++type = AXI4-Lite, addr++_++size = 32,
data++_++size = 32
* Data integrity between the written word and the value returned by the
AXI-Lite subordinate

*Test Procedure*:

[arabic]
. Load SMC++_++OTP++_++JTAG2AXI++_++CAPS (IR=0x1B) and read the 14-bit
CAPS TDR.
[loweralpha]
.. Verify bus++_++type = AXI4-Lite (CAPS bit 0 = 1), addr++_++size = 32,
data++_++size = 32.
. Issue a single write through the SMC OTP AXI-Lite bridge to a chosen
32-bit address.
[loweralpha]
.. Load SMC++_++OTP++_++AXI++_++SINGLE++_++OP (IR=0x1C).
.. Shift in target address, write data, wstrb=all-ones, op=0b10 (Write);
Update-DR.
.. Poll until op status leaves Busy (0b11); verify op=0b00 (OKAY).
. Read back the same address through the SMC OTP AXI-Lite bridge.
[loweralpha]
.. Reload SMC++_++OTP++_++AXI++_++SINGLE++_++OP (IR=0x1C).
.. Shift in target address, data=0, wstrb=0, op=0b01 (Read); Update-DR.
.. Poll op status until not Busy; read returned data and status from the
DR.
. Compare the returned read word against the written value and the
AXI-Lite subordinate model.
. Verify the terminal op status is 0b00 (OKAY) on both the write and the
read.

*Checkers*:

[width="100%",cols="52%,48%",options="header",]
|===
|Checker Type |Description
|`smc++_++otp++_++single++_++wr++_++rd++_++status++_++chk` |Write and
read both reach op=0b00 (OKAY) after leaving Busy

|`smc++_++otp++_++single++_++wr++_++rd++_++rdata++_++chk` |Returned read
word equals the written value and the AXI-Lite model

|`smc++_++otp++_++single++_++wr++_++rd++_++mem++_++chk` |AXI-Lite
subordinate content at the target address matches the issued write
|===

*Pass Criteria*: The write completes OKAY, the read-back returns the
same word with OKAY status, and the memory side effect matches, with
zero scoreboard/reference-model errors.

'''''

returned beat equals the single repeated expected value

===== 3.10 `dtp++_++jtag2axi++_++smc++_++otp++_++axi++_++series++_++write++_++read++_++incr++_++with++_++error++_++test`

*Source Module*:
`dtp++_++jtag2axi++_++smc++_++otp++_++axi++_++rd++_++test`

*BFM / Model*: `ocah++_++jtag++_++vip`, `ocah++_++axi++_++vip`

*Objective*: SMC OTP write-read with error status

*Test Coverage*:

* SMC OTP AXI4-Lite incrementing series that intersects an
error-generating subordinate region
* Error response capture in the op++[++1:0++]++ status (SLVERR 0b01 /
DECERR 0b10)
* Bridge does not hang, corrupt the DR, or lose state after a non-OKAY
response
* Recovery: a subsequent legal read to a good address completes OKAY

*Test Procedure*:

[arabic]
. Configure the SMC OTP AXI-Lite subordinate so a subset of the
incrementing block returns SLVERR/DECERR.
. Issue the incrementing read series through the SMC OTP AXI-Lite
bridge.
[loweralpha]
.. For each address, load SMC++_++OTP++_++AXI++_++SINGLE++_++OP
(IR=0x1C); shift in address{plus}i++*++stride, data=0, wstrb=0, op=0b01
(Read); Update-DR.
.. Poll until not Busy; capture returned data and status per op.
. For addresses in the error region, verify op reflects the expected
error code (0b01/0b10).
. For addresses in the good region, verify op=0b00 (OKAY) and correct
data.
. After the error ops, issue one more legal read to a good address and
confirm OKAY and correct data (recovery).

*Checkers*:

[width="100%",cols="52%,48%",options="header",]
|===
|Checker Type |Description
|`smc++_++otp++_++incr++_++error++_++status++_++chk` |Error-region ops
report the expected SLVERR/DECERR op code

|`smc++_++otp++_++incr++_++error++_++rdata++_++chk` |Good-region beats
match the reference; error beats are not mistaken for valid data

|`smc++_++otp++_++incr++_++error++_++mem++_++chk` |AXI-Lite subordinate
good-region content matches expectations

|`smc++_++otp++_++incr++_++error++_++robustness++_++chk` |Bridge
recovers and a post-error legal read completes OKAY
|===

*Pass Criteria*: Each op reaches its expected terminal status (OKAY or
the configured error), the bridge recovers and accepts a subsequent
legal read, with zero scoreboard/reference-model errors.

'''''

* Repeated SEP OTP AXI4-Lite reads/writes targeting one fixed 32-bit
address (no address advance)
* SEP++_++OTP++_++AXI++_++SINGLE++_++OP (IR=0x22) reissue with an
unchanged address field
* Returned data stability when the last-written value is read multiple
times
* Series-length robustness with no DR/state corruption between repeated
ops

*Test Procedure*:

[arabic]
. Choose a single fixed SEP OTP target address and seed it in the
AXI-Lite subordinate.
. Write the chosen value once through the SEP OTP AXI-Lite bridge.
[loweralpha]
.. Load SEP++_++OTP++_++AXI++_++SINGLE++_++OP (IR=0x22); shift in the
fixed address, write data, wstrb=all-ones, op=0b10 (Write); Update-DR.
.. Poll until not Busy; verify op=0b00 (OKAY).
. Read the same fixed address repeatedly through the bridge.
[loweralpha]
.. For each iteration, load SEP++_++OTP++_++AXI++_++SINGLE++_++OP
(IR=0x22); shift in the fixed address, data=0, wstrb=0, op=0b01 (Read);
Update-DR.
.. Poll until not Busy; capture returned data and status.
. Verify every captured beat equals the single repeated expected value.

*Checkers*:

[width="100%",cols="52%,48%",options="header",]
|===
|Checker Type |Description
|`sep++_++otp++_++series++_++no++_++incr++_++status++_++chk` |Every
fixed-address op reaches op=0b00 (OKAY) after Busy clears

|`sep++_++otp++_++series++_++no++_++incr++_++rdata++_++chk` |Every
returned beat equals the single repeated expected value

|`sep++_++otp++_++series++_++no++_++incr++_++mem++_++chk` |AXI-Lite
subordinate at the fixed address remains the written value across the
series
|===

*Pass Criteria*: All fixed-address ops complete OKAY and every returned
beat equals the repeated expected value, with zero
scoreboard/reference-model errors.

'''''

===== 3.16 `dtp++_++jtag2axi++_++sep++_++otp++_++axi++_++series++_++write++_++read++_++incr++_++with++_++error++_++test`

*Source Module*:
`dtp++_++jtag2axi++_++sep++_++otp++_++axi++_++rd++_++test`

*BFM / Model*: `ocah++_++jtag++_++vip`, `ocah++_++axi++_++vip`

*Objective*: SEP OTP write-read with error status

*Test Coverage*:

* SEP OTP AXI4-Lite incrementing series that intersects an
error-generating subordinate region
* Error response capture in the op++[++1:0++]++ status (SLVERR 0b01 /
DECERR 0b10)
* Bridge does not hang, corrupt the DR, or lose state after a non-OKAY
response
* Recovery: a subsequent legal read to a good address completes OKAY

*Test Procedure*:

[arabic]
. Configure the SEP OTP AXI-Lite subordinate so a subset of the
incrementing block returns SLVERR/DECERR.
. Issue the incrementing read series through the SEP OTP AXI-Lite
bridge.
[loweralpha]
.. For each address, load SEP++_++OTP++_++AXI++_++SINGLE++_++OP
(IR=0x22); shift in address{plus}i++*++stride, data=0, wstrb=0, op=0b01
(Read); Update-DR.
.. Poll until not Busy; capture returned data and status per op.
. For addresses in the error region, verify op reflects the expected
error code (0b01/0b10).
. For addresses in the good region, verify op=0b00 (OKAY) and correct
data.
. After the error ops, issue one more legal read to a good address and
confirm OKAY and correct data (recovery).

*Checkers*:

[width="100%",cols="52%,48%",options="header",]
|===
|Checker Type |Description
|`sep++_++otp++_++incr++_++error++_++status++_++chk` |Error-region ops
report the expected SLVERR/DECERR op code

|`sep++_++otp++_++incr++_++error++_++rdata++_++chk` |Good-region beats
match the reference; error beats are not mistaken for valid data

|`sep++_++otp++_++incr++_++error++_++mem++_++chk` |AXI-Lite subordinate
good-region content matches expectations

|`sep++_++otp++_++incr++_++error++_++robustness++_++chk` |Bridge
recovers and a post-error legal read completes OKAY
|===

*Pass Criteria*: Each op reaches its expected terminal status (OKAY or
the configured error), the bridge recovers and accepts a subsequent
legal read, with zero scoreboard/reference-model errors.

'''''

===== 3.17 `dtp++_++jtag2axi++_++sep++_++otp++_++axi++_++read++_++random++_++ops++_++test`

*Source Module*:
`dtp++_++jtag2axi++_++sep++_++otp++_++axi++_++rd++_++test`

image over the read window.

addresses.

[loweralpha]

random address, data=0, wstrb=0, op=0b01 (Read); Update-DR.
.. Poll op status until not Busy (0b11); capture returned data and
status.
. Compare each returned word with the AXI-Lite subordinate reference
model at that address.

*Checkers*:

[width="100%",cols="52%,48%",options="header",]
|===
|Checker Type |Description
|`sep++_++otp++_++random++_++status++_++chk` |Every randomized read
reaches op=0b00 (OKAY) after Busy clears

|`sep++_++otp++_++random++_++rdata++_++chk` |Each returned word matches
the randomized AXI-Lite reference
|===

*Pass Criteria*: All randomized reads complete OKAY and every returned
word matches the reference model, with zero scoreboard/reference-model
errors.

'''''

===== 3.18 `dtp++_++jtag2axi++_++sep++_++otp++_++axi++_++read++_++security++_++gating++_++test`

*Source Module*:
`dtp++_++jtag2axi++_++sep++_++otp++_++axi++_++rd++_++test`

*BFM / Model*: `ocah++_++jtag++_++vip`, `ocah++_++axi++_++vip`

*Objective*: SEP OTP read security gating

*Test Coverage*:

* `sep++_++otp++_++jtag2axi++_++security++_++disable = !fuse++_++test ++||++ !sep++_++debug ++||++ !soc++_++debug ++||++ !ap++_++debug`
independently gates the SEP OTP read bridge
* Gated JTAG2AXI instructions fall back to BYPASS (1-bit DR); no
AXI-Lite traffic on `axil++_++sep++_++otp++_++jtag++_++req++_++o`
* Normal read operation restored after each required enable is restored

*Test Procedure*:

[arabic]
. Establish a baseline SEP OTP read returning OKAY with expected data
from the AXI-Lite subordinate.
. Drive required enable `feat++_++ctrl++_++i.ap++_++debug=0`; load
SEP++_++OTP++_++AXI++_++SINGLE++_++OP (IR=0x22) and attempt a read.
[loweralpha]
.. Verify the instruction selects BYPASS (DR length 1 bit) and no
AXI-Lite transaction issues on
`axil++_++sep++_++otp++_++jtag++_++req++_++o`.
. Restore `ap++_++debug=1`; verify normal read restored.
. Repeat steps 2-3 for `feat++_++ctrl++_++i.soc++_++debug=0`.
. Repeat steps 2-3 for `feat++_++ctrl++_++i.sep++_++debug=0`.
. Repeat steps 2-3 for `feat++_++ctrl++_++i.fuse++_++test=0`.

*Checkers*:

[width="100%",cols="52%,48%",options="header",]
|===
|Checker Type |Description
|`sep++_++otp++_++rd++_++gate++_++bypass++_++chk` |DR is 1-bit BYPASS
while `ap++_++debug`, `soc++_++debug`, `sep++_++debug`, or
`fuse++_++test` is low

|`sep++_++otp++_++rd++_++gate++_++noaxi++_++chk` |No AXI-Lite
transaction on `axil++_++sep++_++otp++_++jtag++_++req++_++o` while gated

|`sep++_++otp++_++rd++_++gate++_++restore++_++chk` |Normal read with
OKAY status resumes after each bit clears
|===

*Pass Criteria*: Each of `fuse++_++test`, `sep++_++debug`,
`soc++_++debug`, and `ap++_++debug` independently forces BYPASS with no
AXI-Lite traffic when low, and reads resume correctly once restored
high, with zero scoreboard/reference-model errors.

'''''

==== 4. JTAG2AXI Error, Backpressure, CDC, and Decode Scenarios

===== 4.1 `dtp++_++jtag2axi++_++smc++_++axi++_++error++_++single++_++write++_++test`

*Source Module*: `dtp++_++jtag2axi++_++smc++_++axi++_++err++_++test`

*BFM / Model*: `ocah++_++jtag++_++vip`, `ocah++_++axi++_++vip`

*Objective*: SMC fabric single write error

*Test Coverage*:

* JTAG2AXI single-op write through the SMC fabric AXI4 debug bridge
(SMC++_++AXI++_++SINGLE++_++OP, IR=0x28) targeting an error-generating
region
* AXI4 subordinate returns a non-OKAY write response (BRESP = SLVERR or
DECERR) captured in op++[++1:0++]++ (0b10 / 0b01)
* No spurious or partial write side effect lands at the targeted error
address on `axi++_++smc++_++dbg++_++req++_++o`
* Bridge does not hang or corrupt internal state; a subsequent legal
write completes with OKAY

*Test Procedure*:

[arabic]
. Configure the `ocah++_++axi++_++vip` AXI4 subordinate (OcahAxiRam) to
return SLVERR or DECERR on writes to the chosen error region.
. Load SMC++_++AXI++_++SINGLE++_++OP via `OcahJtagTap` (IR=0x28):
[loweralpha]
.. Shift in the error-region target address, write data, wstrb =
all-ones, size, op = 0b10 (Write).
.. Update-DR to launch the AXI4 transaction on
`axi++_++smc++_++dbg++_++req++_++o`.
. Poll the op-status field via Capture-DR/Shift-DR until not Busy (op !=
0b11).
. Verify op++[++1:0++]++ reflects the injected error code (0b01 SLVERR
or 0b10 DECERR) and not OKAY (0b00).
. Confirm no committed write at the error address in the AXI subordinate
model.
. Issue a single-op write to a known-good address; verify OKAY and
correct address/data/wstrb at the subordinate, proving recovery.

*Checkers*:

[width="100%",cols="52%,48%",options="header",]
|===
|Checker Type |Description
|`smc++_++axi++_++err++_++single++_++wr++_++status++_++chk` |Op-status
reports the injected SLVERR/DECERR write-response code

|`smc++_++axi++_++err++_++single++_++wr++_++mem++_++chk` |No unintended
write side effect at the error-region address

|`smc++_++axi++_++err++_++single++_++wr++_++recover++_++chk` |A
subsequent legal write completes with OKAY (no hang/corruption)
|===

*Pass Criteria*: The single write error is reported in the op-status, no
spurious memory update occurs, and the bridge recovers for the next
legal transaction, with zero scoreboard/reference-model errors.

'''''

===== 4.2 `dtp++_++jtag2axi++_++smc++_++axi++_++error++_++single++_++read++_++test`

*Source Module*: `dtp++_++jtag2axi++_++smc++_++axi++_++err++_++test`

(IR=0x28) targeting an error-generating region

DECERR) captured in op++[++1:0++]++ (0b01 / 0b10)

the response is non-OKAY
* Bridge recovers: a following legal read returns correct data with OKAY

[loweralpha]

op = 0b01 (Read).
.. Update-DR to launch the AXI4 read on
`axi++_++smc++_++dbg++_++req++_++o`.
. Poll the op-status via Capture-DR/Shift-DR until not Busy.
. Verify op++[++1:0++]++ reflects the injected error code and that the
read-data field is treated as invalid (ignored / defined value).
. Issue a single-op read to a known-good address holding a reference
value; verify OKAY and matching read data, proving recovery.

[width="100%",cols="52%,48%",options="header",]
|===
|Checker Type |Description
|`smc++_++axi++_++err++_++single++_++rd++_++status++_++chk` |Op-status
reports the injected SLVERR/DECERR read-response code

|`smc++_++axi++_++err++_++single++_++rd++_++rdata++_++chk` |Returned
read data is ignored/held to the defined value on error

|`smc++_++axi++_++err++_++single++_++rd++_++recover++_++chk` |A
subsequent legal read returns the reference data with OKAY
|===

*Pass Criteria*: The single read error is reported in the op-status, the
read-data field is handled per spec, and the bridge recovers for the
next legal read, with zero scoreboard/reference-model errors.

===== 4.8 `dtp++_++jtag2axi++_++smc++_++axi++_++error++_++series++_++incr++_++read++_++with++_++status++_++test`

*Source Module*: `dtp++_++jtag2axi++_++smc++_++axi++_++err++_++test`

*BFM / Model*: `ocah++_++jtag++_++vip`, `ocah++_++axi++_++vip`

*Objective*: SMC fabric incrementing read with error-status TDR

*Test Coverage*:

* JTAG2AXI incrementing-address read series through the SMC fabric AXI4
bridge (IR=0x28) with a fault mid-series
* Sticky error-status TDR scanned out after the series to retrieve the
latched failing-beat fault
* Good beats before the fault return reference data; error-status TDR
identifies the failing beat/address and SLVERR/DECERR code
* Sticky status clears as defined; bridge recovers for a subsequent
legal access

*Test Procedure*:

[arabic]
. Pre-load the `ocah++_++axi++_++vip` subordinate with reference data
and configure a non-OKAY response at one address in the incrementing
range.
. Load SMC++_++AXI++_++SINGLE++_++OP (IR=0x28) and run an
incrementing-address read series of N beats to completion.
. Scan out the sticky error-status TDR and decode the latched
failing-beat index, address, and SLVERR/DECERR code.
. Verify good beats returned the reference data at their incremented
addresses and the latched status matches the injected fault.
. Clear the sticky status, then issue a follow-up legal read; verify
OKAY and correct data, proving recovery.

*Checkers*:

[width="100%",cols="52%,48%",options="header",]
|===
|Checker Type |Description
|`smc++_++axi++_++err++_++incr++_++rd++_++status++_++tdr++_++chk`
|Error-status TDR latches the failing read beat/address and
SLVERR/DECERR code

|`smc++_++axi++_++err++_++incr++_++rd++_++status++_++rdata++_++chk`
|Good beats match the incremented-address reference data

|`smc++_++axi++_++err++_++incr++_++rd++_++status++_++recover++_++chk`
|Sticky status clears and a subsequent legal read returns the reference
data with OKAY
|===

*Pass Criteria*: The sticky error-status TDR correctly reports the
failing read beat and error code, good beats match references, the
status clears, and the bridge recovers, with zero
scoreboard/reference-model errors.

'''''

===== 4.9 `dtp++_++jtag2axi++_++smc++_++axi++_++error++_++security++_++gating++_++test`

*Source Module*: `dtp++_++jtag2axi++_++smc++_++axi++_++err++_++test`

*BFM / Model*: `ocah++_++jtag++_++vip`, `ocah++_++axi++_++vip`

*Objective*: SMC fabric error-path security gating

*Test Coverage*:

* SMC fabric bridge gating:
`smc++_++jtag2axi++_++security++_++disable = !feat++_++ctrl++_++i.soc++_++debug ++||++ !feat++_++ctrl++_++i.ap++_++debug`
* When gated, SMC++_++AXI++_++SINGLE++_++OP (IR=0x28) falls back to
BYPASS (1-bit DR) and issues no AXI traffic on
`axi++_++smc++_++dbg++_++req++_++o`, even on error-path attempts
* Each required enable low (`soc++_++debug`, `ap++_++debug`) disables
the bridge; restoring it high restores operation
* Error-path read/write attempts while gated produce no subordinate
access and no error-status update

*Test Procedure*:

[arabic]
. With the subordinate configured to return errors on the target region,
drive required enable `feat++_++ctrl++_++i.ap++_++debug` = 0 to gate the
bridge.
. Load SMC++_++AXI++_++SINGLE++_++OP (IR=0x28) and attempt an
error-region read and write:
[loweralpha]
.. Verify the instruction selects BYPASS (1-bit DR).
.. Verify no AXI transaction is issued on
`axi++_++smc++_++dbg++_++req++_++o` and no error-status latches.
. Restore `feat++_++ctrl++_++i.ap++_++debug` = 1; verify a normal (and
error-path) JTAG2AXI access resumes and the error is reported as
expected.
. Repeat steps 1–3 with `feat++_++ctrl++_++i.soc++_++debug` = 0 to
confirm it independently gates the bridge.
. Confirm that with all required enables high, ungated error-path
accesses complete and report SLVERR/DECERR normally.

*Checkers*:

[width="100%",cols="52%,48%",options="header",]
|===
|Checker Type |Description
|`smc++_++axi++_++err++_++gating++_++bypass++_++chk` |Gated instruction
selects BYPASS (1-bit DR) with `soc++_++debug` or `ap++_++debug` set

|`smc++_++axi++_++err++_++gating++_++no++_++traffic++_++chk` |No AXI
traffic on `axi++_++smc++_++dbg++_++req++_++o` while gated

|`smc++_++axi++_++err++_++gating++_++restore++_++chk` |Restoring each
required enable restores normal/error-path operation
|===

*Pass Criteria*: Either required enable low forces BYPASS with no AXI
traffic on the error path, and operation is fully restored after each
enable is restored, with zero scoreboard/reference-model errors.

'''''

===== 4.10 `dtp++_++jtag2axi++_++smc++_++otp++_++axi++_++error++_++single++_++write++_++test`

*Source Module*:
`dtp++_++jtag2axi++_++smc++_++otp++_++axi++_++err++_++test`

*BFM / Model*: `ocah++_++jtag++_++vip`, `ocah++_++axi++_++vip`

*Objective*: SMC OTP single write error

*Test Coverage*:

* JTAG2AXI single-op write through the SMC OTP AXI4-Lite debug bridge
(SMC++_++OTP++_++AXI++_++SINGLE++_++OP, IR=0x1C) targeting an error
region
* AXI4-Lite subordinate returns a non-OKAY write response
(SLVERR/DECERR) captured in op++[++1:0++]++
* No spurious write side effect lands at the targeted error address on
`axil++_++smc++_++otp++_++jtag++_++req++_++o`
* Bridge recovers: a subsequent legal write completes with OKAY

*Test Procedure*:

[arabic]
. Configure the `ocah++_++axi++_++vip` AXI4-Lite subordinate (AXI-Lite
wrapper) to return SLVERR/DECERR on writes to the error region.
. Load SMC++_++OTP++_++AXI++_++SINGLE++_++OP via `OcahJtagTap`
(IR=0x1C):
[loweralpha]
.. Shift in the 32-bit error-region address, write data, wstrb =
all-ones, op = 0b10 (Write).
.. Update-DR to launch the AXI4-Lite transaction on
`axil++_++smc++_++otp++_++jtag++_++req++_++o`.
. Poll the op-status via Capture-DR/Shift-DR until not Busy.
. Verify op++[++1:0++]++ reflects the injected error code (0b01 SLVERR
or 0b10 DECERR), not OKAY.
. Confirm no committed write at the error address in the subordinate
model.
. Issue a single-op write to a known-good address; verify OKAY and
correct address/data, proving recovery.

*Checkers*:

[width="100%",cols="52%,48%",options="header",]
|===
|Checker Type |Description
|`smc++_++otp++_++axi++_++err++_++single++_++wr++_++status++_++chk`
|Op-status reports the injected SLVERR/DECERR write-response code

|`smc++_++otp++_++axi++_++err++_++single++_++wr++_++mem++_++chk` |No
unintended write side effect at the error-region address

|`smc++_++otp++_++axi++_++err++_++single++_++wr++_++recover++_++chk` |A
subsequent legal write completes with OKAY (no hang/corruption)
|===

*Pass Criteria*: The single write error is reported in the op-status, no
spurious update occurs on the AXI-Lite subordinate, and the bridge
recovers, with zero scoreboard/reference-model errors.

'''''

subsequent legal read returns the reference data with OKAY
|===

* When gated, SMC++_++OTP++_++AXI++_++SINGLE++_++OP (IR=0x1C) falls back
to BYPASS (1-bit DR) and issues no AXI-Lite traffic on
`axil++_++smc++_++otp++_++jtag++_++req++_++o`, even on error-path
attempts
* Each required enable low (`fuse++_++test`, `soc++_++debug`,
`ap++_++debug`) disables the bridge; restoring it high restores
operation
* Error-path read/write attempts while gated produce no subordinate
access and no error-status update

*Test Procedure*:

[arabic]
. With the AXI-Lite subordinate configured to return errors on the
target region, drive required enable
`feat++_++ctrl++_++i.ap++_++debug` = 0 to gate the bridge.
. Load SMC++_++OTP++_++AXI++_++SINGLE++_++OP (IR=0x1C) and attempt an
error-region read and write:
[loweralpha]
.. Verify the instruction selects BYPASS (1-bit DR).
.. Verify no AXI-Lite transaction is issued on
`axil++_++smc++_++otp++_++jtag++_++req++_++o` and no error-status
latches.
. Restore `feat++_++ctrl++_++i.ap++_++debug` = 1; verify
normal/error-path operation resumes and the error is reported as
expected.
. Repeat steps 1–3 for `feat++_++ctrl++_++i.soc++_++debug` = 0 and
`feat++_++ctrl++_++i.fuse++_++test` = 0, confirming each independently
gates the bridge.
. Confirm that with all required enables high, ungated error-path
accesses complete and report SLVERR/DECERR normally.

*Checkers*:

[width="100%",cols="52%,48%",options="header",]
|===
|Checker Type |Description
|`smc++_++otp++_++axi++_++err++_++gating++_++bypass++_++chk` |Gated
instruction selects BYPASS (1-bit DR) for each of
`fuse++_++test`/`soc++_++debug`/`ap++_++debug`

|`smc++_++otp++_++axi++_++err++_++gating++_++no++_++traffic++_++chk` |No
AXI-Lite traffic on `axil++_++smc++_++otp++_++jtag++_++req++_++o` while
gated

|`smc++_++otp++_++axi++_++err++_++gating++_++restore++_++chk` |Restoring
each required enable restores normal/error-path operation
|===

*Pass Criteria*: Any required enable low forces BYPASS with no AXI-Lite
traffic on the error path, and operation is fully restored after each
enable is restored, with zero scoreboard/reference-model errors.

'''''

===== 4.19 `dtp++_++jtag2axi++_++sep++_++otp++_++axi++_++error++_++single++_++write++_++test`

*Source Module*:
`dtp++_++jtag2axi++_++sep++_++otp++_++axi++_++err++_++test`

*BFM / Model*: `ocah++_++jtag++_++vip`, `ocah++_++axi++_++vip`

*Objective*: SEP OTP single write error

*Test Coverage*:

* JTAG2AXI single-op write through the SEP OTP AXI4-Lite debug bridge
(SEP++_++OTP++_++AXI++_++SINGLE++_++OP, IR=0x22) targeting an error
region
* AXI4-Lite subordinate returns a non-OKAY write response
(SLVERR/DECERR) captured in op++[++1:0++]++
* No spurious write side effect lands at the targeted error address on
`axil++_++sep++_++otp++_++jtag++_++req++_++o`
* Bridge recovers: a subsequent legal write completes with OKAY

*Test Procedure*:

[arabic]
. Configure the `ocah++_++axi++_++vip` AXI4-Lite subordinate to return
SLVERR/DECERR on writes to the error region.
. Load SEP++_++OTP++_++AXI++_++SINGLE++_++OP via `OcahJtagTap`
(IR=0x22):
[loweralpha]
.. Shift in the 32-bit error-region address, write data, wstrb =
all-ones, op = 0b10 (Write).
.. Update-DR to launch the AXI4-Lite transaction on
`axil++_++sep++_++otp++_++jtag++_++req++_++o`.
. Poll the op-status via Capture-DR/Shift-DR until not Busy.
. Verify op++[++1:0++]++ reflects the injected error code (0b01 SLVERR
or 0b10 DECERR), not OKAY.
. Confirm no committed write at the error address in the subordinate
model.
. Issue a single-op write to a known-good address; verify OKAY and
correct address/data, proving recovery.

*Checkers*:

[width="100%",cols="52%,48%",options="header",]
|===
|Checker Type |Description
|`sep++_++otp++_++axi++_++err++_++single++_++wr++_++status++_++chk`
|Op-status reports the injected SLVERR/DECERR write-response code

|`sep++_++otp++_++axi++_++err++_++single++_++wr++_++mem++_++chk` |No
unintended write side effect at the error-region address

|`sep++_++otp++_++axi++_++err++_++single++_++wr++_++recover++_++chk` |A
subsequent legal write completes with OKAY (no hang/corruption)
|===

*Pass Criteria*: The single write error is reported in the op-status, no
spurious update occurs on the AXI-Lite subordinate, and the bridge
recovers, with zero scoreboard/reference-model errors.

'''''

`axil++_++sep++_++otp++_++jtag++_++req++_++o`.
. Poll the op-status after each beat until not Busy.

failing beat reports SLVERR/DECERR, and the address progression matches

beats commit at correctly-incremented addresses

|`sep++_++otp++_++axi++_++err++_++incr++_++wr++_++recover++_++chk` |A
subsequent legal write completes with OKAY
|===

*Objective*: AW before W channel skew

*Test Coverage*:

* Maximum AW/W channel skew on the JTAG2AXI single-op write path with AW
ready asserted while W ready is held low
* Reachable `AXI++_++SEND++_++ADDR++_++W -++>++ AXI++_++WAIT++_++BRESP`
FSM transition on `axi++_++state++_++q++_++tclk`
* AXI-Lite `aw++_++valid++_++stable` / `w++_++valid++_++stable` and
payload-stability assertions while ready is low
* Intended to support a documented waiver for
`AXI++_++SEND++_++DATA++_++W` should that state remain structurally
unreachable (AW{plus}W are pushed atomically through the per-channel
`axi++_++cdc++_++clearable` FIFOs, so the FSM history is expected to
skip it)

*Test Procedure*:

[arabic]
. Drive a JTAG2AXI single-op write through `OcahJtagTap`:
[loweralpha]
.. Program the bridge address and data via the primary TAP and launch
the write op.
.. Configure the `ocah++_++axi++_++vip` subordinate to skew the
channels: accept AW (ready high) while holding W ready low for an
extended interval.
. Observe the bridge FSM and channels under skew:
[loweralpha]
.. Sample the FSM history mirror and confirm the sequence
`AXI++_++IDLE -++>++ AXI++_++SEND++_++ADDR++_++W -++>++ AXI++_++WAIT++_++BRESP -++>++ AXI++_++UPDATE++_++STATUS -++>++ AXI++_++IDLE`.
.. Confirm `axi++_++state++_++q++_++tclk == AXI++_++SEND++_++DATA++_++W`
is never observed at any sample point.
.. While W ready is low, confirm AW/W valid and payload remain stable
and do not deassert.
. Release the W-channel backpressure and let the write drain.
. Verify the write completes with an OKAY response and read back the
written location to confirm the value landed.

*Checkers*:

[width="100%",cols="52%,48%",options="header",]
|===
|Checker Type |Description
|`j2a++_++bp++_++fsm++_++chk` |FSM traverses
`AXI++_++SEND++_++ADDR++_++W -++>++ AXI++_++WAIT++_++BRESP` and never
enters `AXI++_++SEND++_++DATA++_++W`

|`j2a++_++bp++_++stable++_++chk` |AW/W valid and payload remain stable
while the subordinate holds ready low

|`j2a++_++bp++_++status++_++chk` |Final JTAG2AXI completion status is
OKAY and read-back data matches the written value
|===

*Pass Criteria*: The reachable maximum-skew path and AXI-Lite stable
assertions are exercised, the observed FSM history supports a waiver for
the (expected structurally-unreachable) `AXI++_++SEND++_++DATA++_++W`
state, and the write completes correctly with zero
scoreboard/reference-model errors.

'''''

===== 4.29 `dtp++_++jtag2axi++_++backpressure++_++long++_++stall++_++test`

*Source Module*: `dtp++_++jtag2axi++_++backpressure++_++test`

*BFM / Model*: `ocah++_++jtag++_++vip`, `ocah++_++axi++_++vip`

*Objective*: Long ready stalls

*Test Coverage*:

* Long subordinate ready stalls on AW, W, AR, and R channels exercised
one channel at a time and combined
* Real success hits on the AXI-Lite `aw++_++valid++_++stable` /
`w++_++valid++_++stable` / `ar++_++valid++_++stable` and corresponding
payload `++*_++stable` assertions
* Reachable JTAG2AXI FSM dwell in `AXI++_++SEND++_++ADDR++_++W` /
`AXI++_++WAIT++_++BRESP` (write) and the read-issue / wait states under
prolonged stall
* No premature timeout, abort, or status corruption while a legal stall
is in progress

*Test Procedure*:

[arabic]
. Set up stall stimulus on the `ocah++_++axi++_++vip` subordinate:
[loweralpha]
.. Configure long, deterministic ready-low intervals on AW/W (write
path) and AR/R (read path) that remain inside the architectural stall
budget.
. Issue write traffic through `OcahJtagTap`:
[loweralpha]
.. Launch a single-op write while AW/W ready are stalled; observe
valid/payload hold stable across the full stall window.
.. Release the stall and confirm OKAY completion.
. Issue read traffic through `OcahJtagTap`:
[loweralpha]
.. Launch a single-op read while AR ready (then R ready) is stalled;
observe AR valid/address hold stable.
.. Release the stall and confirm the returned data matches the reference
model.
. Verify the bridge does not declare a timeout or error during any legal
stall and that each operation reaches its expected terminal status after
release.

*Checkers*:

[width="100%",cols="52%,48%",options="header",]
|===
|Checker Type |Description
|`j2a++_++long++_++stall++_++assert++_++chk` |AW/W/AR valid and payload
`++*_++stable` assertions hold across the full stall window

|`j2a++_++long++_++stall++_++status++_++chk` |No premature
timeout/abort; each op reaches its expected terminal status after stall
release

|`j2a++_++long++_++stall++_++data++_++chk` |Post-stall write-back and
read-data compare against the reference model
|===

*Pass Criteria*: All AW/W/AR stable assertions receive real success hits
under long stalls, no operation times out or aborts during a legal
stall, and every read/write completes correctly after release with zero
scoreboard/reference-model errors.

'''''

===== 4.30 `dtp++_++jtag2axi++_++backpressure++_++abort++_++at++_++data++_++w++_++test`

*Source Module*: `dtp++_++jtag2axi++_++backpressure++_++test`

*BFM / Model*: `ocah++_++jtag++_++vip`, `ocah++_++axi++_++vip`

*Objective*: Reset abort around data phase

*Test Coverage*:

* Reset/abort asserted from reachable JTAG2AXI FSM states while a write
is stalled at the data phase
* In-flight bridge state and per-channel `axi++_++cdc++_++clearable`
FIFO contents are flushed on abort
* No partial or stale write escapes to the subordinate after the abort
* Clean recovery: a subsequent legal write/read completes normally from
`AXI++_++IDLE`

*Test Procedure*:

[arabic]
. Stage a stalled write through `OcahJtagTap`:
[loweralpha]
.. Launch a single-op write and hold the subordinate W ready low so the
bridge dwells in the reachable write FSM states
(`AXI++_++SEND++_++ADDR++_++W` / `AXI++_++WAIT++_++BRESP`).
. Assert reset/abort mid-flight:
[loweralpha]
.. Drive the JTAG2AXI reset/abort while the operation is held,
exercising the abort transition out of the reachable FSM state.
.. Sample the FSM history and confirm the state returns to
`AXI++_++IDLE` and never lands in `AXI++_++SEND++_++DATA++_++W`.
. Confirm containment:
[loweralpha]
.. Verify the `ocah++_++axi++_++vip` subordinate observed no completed
write (no escaped partial transaction) for the aborted op.
.. Verify the bridge status reports the abort/idle condition rather than
a spurious OKAY.
. Issue a fresh clean write and read-back to confirm full recovery.

*Checkers*:

[width="100%",cols="52%,48%",options="header",]
|===
|Checker Type |Description
|`j2a++_++abort++_++fsm++_++chk` |Abort drives the FSM back to
`AXI++_++IDLE` from a reachable state without entering
`AXI++_++SEND++_++DATA++_++W`

|`j2a++_++abort++_++escape++_++chk` |No partial/stale write reaches the
subordinate for the aborted operation

|`j2a++_++abort++_++recovery++_++chk` |A subsequent legal write/read
completes correctly after the abort
|===

*Pass Criteria*: The reset/abort transition fires from a reachable FSM
state, in-flight state is fully flushed with no escaped write, and the
bridge recovers to service a clean follow-on access with zero
scoreboard/reference-model errors.

'''''

===== 4.31 `dtp++_++jtag2axi++_++cdc++_++clear++_++abort++_++narrow++_++reset++_++mid++_++xaction++_++test`

mid-transaction, exercising the `cdc++_++reset++_++ctrlr++_++half`
intermediate clear/isolate recovery states.
.. Sample the CDC controller state and confirm it passes through the
expected intermediate recovery states rather than hanging.
. Verify post-pulse status reports abort/idle and the CDC FIFOs are
emptied/cleared.
. Issue a follow-on read/write and confirm it completes with the
expected status and data.

[width="100%",cols="52%,48%",options="header",]

|`cdc++_++reset++_++ctrlr++_++half` passes through the expected
intermediate clear/isolate recovery states

|`j2a++_++cdc++_++narrow++_++status++_++chk` |Post-pulse bridge status
converges to a defined abort/idle condition

completes correctly after the narrow reset

*Pass Criteria*: The narrow reset pulse drives
`cdc++_++reset++_++ctrlr++_++half` through its reachable intermediate

===== 4.32 `dtp++_++jtag2axi++_++cdc++_++clear++_++abort++_++back++_++to++_++back++_++reset++_++test`

*Source Module*: `dtp++_++jtag2axi++_++cdc++_++clear++_++abort++_++test`

clock domains

has fully settled, forcing CDC reset re-entry.

ISOLATE / CLEAR recovery states and does not deadlock on re-entry.
. Verify all bridge FIFOs and FSMs settle to a clean idle after the
reset burst.

its expected terminal status with correct data.

[width="100%",cols="52%,48%",options="header",]
|===
|Checker Type |Description
|`j2a++_++cdc++_++b2b++_++state++_++chk` |CDC controller exercises
IDLE/ISOLATE/CLEAR recovery paths through reset re-entry without
deadlock

|`j2a++_++cdc++_++b2b++_++settle++_++chk` |All bridge FIFOs/FSMs return
to a clean idle after the back-to-back reset burst

|`j2a++_++cdc++_++b2b++_++recovery++_++chk` |Post-recovery reads/writes
complete correctly with expected status and data
|===

*Pass Criteria*: Back-to-back CDC resets exercise the IDLE/ISOLATE/CLEAR
recovery paths and reset re-entry without deadlock, all bridge state
settles cleanly, and subsequent operations complete correctly with zero
scoreboard/reference-model errors.

===== 4.33 `dtp++_++jtag2axi++_++decode++_++error++_++decerr++_++write++_++test`

*Source Module*: `dtp++_++jtag2axi++_++decode++_++err++_++test`

*BFM / Model*: `ocah++_++axi++_++vip`, OCAH-local XTRIG monitor

*Objective*: CTN default-slave write DECERR

*Test Coverage*:

* CTN AXI-Lite xbar default-slave write DECERR path triggered by a write
to an unmapped address
* Xbar `default++_*_++mst++_++port++[_++en++]++` write-decode branch and
default-slave response generation
* No mapped CTN register receives a side effect from the unmapped write
* Bridge recovers and accepts a subsequent legal mapped write

*Test Procedure*:

[arabic]
. Issue an unmapped write:
[loweralpha]
.. Drive a JTAG2AXI write to an address with no valid CTN xbar slave so
it routes to the default DECERR slave.
. Verify the error response and monitor:
[loweralpha]
.. Confirm the write completion reports DECERR.
.. Confirm the OCAH-local XTRIG monitor observes the decode miss /
default-slave routing event.
. Verify no side effects:
[loweralpha]
.. Compare all mapped CTN register/memory contents against the reference
model and confirm none changed.
. Issue a follow-on legal mapped write and confirm it completes with
OKAY and the expected value lands.

*Checkers*:

[width="100%",cols="52%,48%",options="header",]
|===
|Checker Type |Description
|`j2a++_++decerr++_++wr++_++status++_++chk` |Unmapped write reports
DECERR via the default-slave path

|`j2a++_++decerr++_++wr++_++mem++_++chk` |No mapped CTN register/memory
side effect from the unmapped write

|`j2a++_++decerr++_++wr++_++recovery++_++chk` |A subsequent legal mapped
write completes with OKAY
|===

*Pass Criteria*: The unmapped write routes to the CTN xbar default
DECERR slave and returns DECERR, no mapped register is disturbed, the
XTRIG monitor witnesses the decode miss, and a follow-on legal write
succeeds with zero scoreboard/reference-model errors.

'''''

===== 4.35 `dtp++_++jtag2axi++_++decode++_++error++_++mixed++_++test`

[loweralpha]
.. Confirm each mapped access completes with OKAY and the expected
data/side effect.
.. Confirm each unmapped access completes with DECERR via the default
slave.

issue order.

[loweralpha]

*Pass Criteria*: The mapped/unmapped interleave exercises both the

===== 4.36 `dtp++_++jtag2axi++_++series++_++corner++_++all++_++bridges++_++test`

*Source Module*: `dtp++_++jtag2axi++_++series++_++corner++_++test`

*BFM / Model*: `ocah++_++jtag++_++vip`, `ocah++_++axi++_++vip`

*Objective*: Series corner cases across all bridges

*Test Coverage*:

* Series-operation FIFO depth corners (minimum, maximum, and
full/near-full) across all three JTAG2AXI bridges
* Sticky status accumulation and clear behavior over multi-beat series
operations
* Address bookkeeping for fixed and incrementing series, including
aligned and unaligned start addresses
* Per-bridge independence: each enabled bridge tracks its own series
state without aliasing the others
* Read pipelining via `SERIES++_++CTRL.pl++_++depth`: with
`pl++_++depth=N`, up to `N{plus}1` reads may be outstanding; the first
`N{plus}1` reads return the `DEADBEEF` marker and subsequent reads
return real data in deterministic queued order; `pl++_++depth` swept up
to each bridge’s reported `(rd/wr)++_++pl++_++depth` cap

*Test Procedure*:

[arabic]
. For each of the three enabled JTAG2AXI bridges, drive a sweep of
series operations through `OcahJtagTap`:
[loweralpha]
.. Minimum-length and maximum-length series to exercise the series FIFO
depth corners.
.. Aligned and unaligned start addresses to exercise address
bookkeeping.
.. Fixed and incrementing series to exercise both address-progression
modes.
.. Read series with `SERIES++_++CTRL.pl++_++depth` swept from 0 to the
bridge’s reported pipeline-depth cap, issuing `pl++_++depth{plus}1`
outstanding reads.
. Capture per-operation results from the `ocah++_++axi++_++vip`
subordinate:
[loweralpha]
.. Confirm the series FIFO accepts each beat up to its depth and reports
the expected status at the FIFO corners.
.. Confirm sticky status accumulates correctly across the series and
clears as defined.
.. Confirm the final address matches the expected fixed/incrementing
progression for each series length.
.. For the pipelined reads, confirm the first `pl++_++depth{plus}1`
reads return the `DEADBEEF` marker and subsequent reads return the
subordinate data in deterministic queued order.
. Verify per-bridge isolation: repeat across all three bridges and
confirm no bridge’s series state, status, or address bookkeeping aliases
another.

*Checkers*:

[width="100%",cols="52%,48%",options="header",]
|===
|Checker Type |Description
|`j2a++_++series++_++fifo++_++chk` |Series FIFO behaves correctly at
minimum/maximum/full depth corners on each bridge

|`j2a++_++series++_++status++_++chk` |Sticky status accumulates and
clears correctly across each series operation

|`j2a++_++series++_++addr++_++chk` |Final address matches the expected
fixed/incrementing progression for aligned and unaligned starts

|`j2a++_++series++_++pldepth++_++chk` |With `pl++_++depth=N`, the first
`N{plus}1` reads return `DEADBEEF` and later reads return real data in
queued order
|===

*Pass Criteria*: All series corner cases (FIFO depth, sticky status,
address bookkeeping, and `pl++_++depth` read pipelining with DEADBEEF
marker behavior) complete as expected on each of the three bridges, with
no cross-bridge aliasing and zero scoreboard/reference-model errors.

'''''

==== 5. STAP, 3DCR, and iJTAG Scenarios

===== 5.1 `dtp++_++ijtag++_++sib++_++all++_++off++_++test`

*Source Module*: `dtp++_++ijtag++_++sib++_++all++_++off++_++test`

*BFM / Model*: `ocah++_++jtag++_++vip`, OCAH-local iJTAG SIB model

*Objective*: All SIBs OFF

*Test Coverage*:

* SELECT++_++IJTAG (IR=0x1A) with every SIB control bit driven to 0
yields the minimum-length scan chain
* DR chain length equals the SIB-control bits only (3 bits for
sec++_++dft, dft, dfd) with no instrument registers inserted
* None of `jtag++_++dfd++_++host++_++scan++_++ctrl++_++o`,
`jtag++_++dft++_++host++_++scan++_++ctrl++_++o`,
`jtag++_++dft++_++secure++_++host++_++scan++_++ctrl++_++o` assert during
Shift-DR
* Closed SIBs bypass their downstream instruments so no instrument
capture/update occurs

*Test Procedure*:

[arabic]
. Load SELECT++_++IJTAG (IR=0x1A) into the PTAP via `OcahJtagTap`.
. Shift a SIB pattern with all three SIB bits = 0 and pulse Update-DR.
. Measure the active DR chain length.
[loweralpha]
.. Shift a known marker pattern and count the bit delay through the
chain.
.. Verify the length equals the 3 SIB-control bits only (no instrument
register widths added).
. During Shift-DR, sample all three scan-control outputs and verify none
asserts (instruments are bypassed).
. Compare captured/update data against the OCAH-local iJTAG SIB model
for the all-off configuration.

*Checkers*:

[width="100%",cols="52%,48%",options="header",]
|===
|Checker Type |Description
|`sib++_++all++_++off++_++len++_++chk` |DR chain length equals
SIB-control bits only with all instruments bypassed

|`sib++_++all++_++off++_++ctrl++_++chk` |No DFD/DFT/secure-DFT
scan-control output asserts during Shift-DR

|`sib++_++all++_++off++_++data++_++chk` |Captured/update data matches
the SIB reference model for the all-off path
|===

*Pass Criteria*: Chain length collapses to the SIB-control bits, all
instrument scan controls stay inactive, and observed data matches the
SIB reference model, with zero scoreboard/reference-model errors.

'''''

===== 5.2 `dtp++_++ijtag++_++sib++_++all++_++on++_++test`

*Source Module*: `dtp++_++ijtag++_++sib++_++all++_++on++_++test`

*BFM / Model*: `ocah++_++jtag++_++vip`, OCAH-local iJTAG SIB model

*Objective*: All SIBs ON

*Test Coverage*:

* SELECT++_++IJTAG (IR=0x1A) with all SIB bits = 1 produces the
full-length iJTAG scan chain
* DR chain length equals sum of SIB bits plus all three instrument
register widths (3 SIBs {plus} 3 instruments)
* All three scan controls
(`jtag++_++dfd++_++host++_++scan++_++ctrl++_++o`,
`jtag++_++dft++_++host++_++scan++_++ctrl++_++o`,
`jtag++_++dft++_++secure++_++host++_++scan++_++ctrl++_++o`) assert
during Shift-DR
* `feat++_++ctrl++_++i.ap++_++debug=0` forces all three SIBs closed even
when the scan pattern requests all-ON, collapsing the chain
* Access is restored when `ap++_++debug` returns to `1`

*Test Procedure*:

[arabic]
. Load SELECT++_++IJTAG (IR=0x1A) via `OcahJtagTap`.
. Shift a pattern enabling all SIBs (all bits = 1); Update-DR.
. Measure DR chain length by shifting a known marker and counting the
delay.
[loweralpha]
.. Verify length equals the sum of SIB bits plus all three instrument
register widths.
. Verify all three host scan-control outputs assert during Shift-DR and
instrument data is captured correctly per the SIB model.
. Security gating:
[loweralpha]
.. Drive required enable `feat++_++ctrl++_++i.ap++_++debug=0` (disables
DFD, secure DFT, non-secure DFT).
.. Re-shift the all-ON pattern; Update-DR.
.. Verify chain length collapses to SIB bits only and no instrument scan
control asserts.
. Clear `ap++_++debug=0`; re-shift all-ON and confirm full chain and all
scan controls restored.

*Checkers*:

[width="100%",cols="52%,48%",options="header",]
|===
|Checker Type |Description
|`sib++_++all++_++on++_++len++_++chk` |Full chain length matches sum of
SIB bits plus all three instrument widths

|`sib++_++all++_++on++_++ctrl++_++chk` |All three host scan-control
outputs assert during Shift-DR

|`sib++_++all++_++on++_++gate++_++chk` |`ap++_++debug`=1 forces every
SIB closed; chain collapses despite all-ON request

|`sib++_++all++_++on++_++restore++_++chk` |Full chain and scan controls
restored after `ap++_++debug` clears
|===

*Pass Criteria*: The all-ON chain reaches full length with every scan
control active, collapses to SIB bits when `ap++_++debug=0`, and
recovers when restored, with zero scoreboard/reference-model errors.

'''''

===== 5.3 `dtp++_++ijtag++_++sib++_++random++_++test`

*Source Module*: `dtp++_++ijtag++_++sib++_++random++_++test`

*BFM / Model*: `ocah++_++jtag++_++vip`, OCAH-local iJTAG SIB model

*Objective*: Exhaustive/random SIB patterns

*Test Coverage*:

* All 8 SIB on/off patterns (sec++_++dft x dft x dfd) walked
exhaustively to close `ijtag++_++sib++_++pattern` coverage
* Predicted chain length matches sum of (SIB bit {plus} instrument width
when SIB ON and ungated, else SIB bit only)
* Only enabled, ungated instruments receive their host scan-control
assertions
* Randomized `feat++_++ctrl++_++i` (`soc++_++debug`, `ap++_++debug`,
`sep++_++debug`, `fuse++_++test`) forces gated SIBs closed regardless of
scan pattern
* Capture/update data tracks the SIB reference model across mixed
configurations

*Test Procedure*:

[arabic]
. Load SELECT++_++IJTAG (IR=0x1A) via `OcahJtagTap`.
. Walk all 8 SIB patterns exhaustively:
[loweralpha]
.. Shift each (sec++_++dft, dft, dfd) pattern; Update-DR.
.. Measure chain length and verify it equals SIB bits plus the widths of
enabled instruments.
.. Verify scan-control outputs assert only for the enabled instruments.
. For N additional randomized iterations (e.g., 20):
[loweralpha]
.. Drive random `feat++_++ctrl++_++i` fields and a random SIB pattern;
Update-DR.
.. Verify gated SIBs (those with `security++_++disable++_++i` asserted)
stay closed despite the requested pattern.
.. Verify chain length accounts for forced-closed SIBs and only
non-gated enabled instruments receive scan control.
. Compare all captured/update data against the OCAH-local SIB model for
each configuration.

*Checkers*:

[width="100%",cols="52%,48%",options="header",]
|===
|Checker Type |Description
|`sib++_++random++_++len++_++chk` |Chain length matches predicted
SIB-bit {plus} ungated-instrument-width sum

|`sib++_++random++_++ctrl++_++chk` |Scan control asserts only for
enabled, ungated instruments

|`sib++_++random++_++gate++_++chk` |Security-gated SIBs forced closed
under random `feat++_++ctrl++_++i`

|`sib++_++random++_++data++_++chk` |Captured/update data matches SIB
reference model across all patterns
|===

*Pass Criteria*: All 8 SIB patterns plus randomized security
combinations yield correct chain lengths, scan-control assertions, and
gating behavior matching the reference model, with zero
scoreboard/reference-model errors.

'''''

===== 5.4 `dtp++_++ijtag++_++dft++_++test`

*Source Module*: `dtp++_++ijtag++_++dft++_++test`

*BFM / Model*: `ocah++_++jtag++_++vip`, OCAH-local iJTAG SIB model

*Objective*: DFT secure/non-secure access

*Test Coverage*:

* Independent access to the secure and non-secure DFT instruments via
their SIBs (SELECT++_++IJTAG, IR=0x1A)
* Both DFT SIBs may be open simultaneously (no hardware mutual
exclusion); chain length sums both instruments
* `dft++_++nonsecure++_++security++_++disable = !soc++_++debug ++||++ !ap++_++debug`
gates the non-secure DFT SIB
* `dft++_++secure++_++security++_++disable = !fuse++_++test ++||++ !sep++_++debug ++||++ !soc++_++debug ++||++ !ap++_++debug`
gates the secure DFT SIB
* Driving `soc++_++debug` low gates both SIBs; driving `sep++_++debug`
or `fuse++_++test` low gates only the secure DFT SIB

*Test Procedure*:

[arabic]
. Load SELECT++_++IJTAG (IR=0x1A); enable the non-secure DFT SIB;
Update-DR.
[loweralpha]
.. Verify `jtag++_++dft++_++host++_++scan++_++ctrl++_++o` asserts and
shift a test pattern through the non-secure chain.
. Disable non-secure, enable secure DFT SIB; Update-DR.
[loweralpha]
.. Verify `jtag++_++dft++_++secure++_++host++_++scan++_++ctrl++_++o`
asserts and shift a test pattern through the secure chain.
. Enable both DFT SIBs; Update-DR; verify both scan controls assert and
chain length equals the sum of both instruments.
. Non-secure gating: drive `feat++_++ctrl++_++i.soc++_++debug=0`; verify
both SIBs forced closed (soc++_++debug appears in both formulas);
restore.
. Secure-only gating via `sep++_++debug=0`: verify secure DFT SIB forced
closed while non-secure remains openable; restore.
. Secure-only gating via `fuse++_++test=0`: verify secure DFT SIB forced
closed while non-secure remains openable; restore and confirm both SIBs
restored.

*Checkers*:

[width="100%",cols="52%,48%",options="header",]
|===
|Checker Type |Description
|`dft++_++access++_++chk` |Secure and non-secure DFT chains each
accessible via their SIB with correct scan controls

|`dft++_++parallel++_++chk` |Both DFT SIBs open simultaneously; chain
length sums both instruments

|`dft++_++nonsecure++_++gate++_++chk` |Non-secure DFT SIB forced closed
under `soc++_++debug ++\++{vbar}++\++{vbar} ap++_++debug`

|`dft++_++secure++_++gate++_++chk` |Secure DFT SIB forced closed when
any required enable is low

|`dft++_++gate++_++restore++_++chk` |Both DFT SIBs restored after
required `feat++_++ctrl++_++i` enables are high
|===

*Pass Criteria*: Secure and non-secure DFT instruments are independently
reachable in the same scan flow, each gated by its own security formula
and restored when required enables are high, with zero
scoreboard/reference-model errors.

'''''

===== 5.5 `dtp++_++ijtag++_++dfd++_++test`

*Source Module*: `dtp++_++ijtag++_++dfd++_++test`

*BFM / Model*: `ocah++_++jtag++_++vip`, OCAH-local iJTAG SIB model

*Test Coverage*:

and data shifts correctly through
`jtag++_++dfd++_++host++_++scan++_++out++_++o` /
`jtag++_++dfd++_++host++_++scan++_++in++_++i`

and read it back through `jtag++_++dfd++_++host++_++scan++_++in++_++i`

SIB.
[loweralpha]
.. Verify the SIB stays closed (`security++_++disable++_++i` prevents it
latching ON) and `jtag++_++dfd++_++host++_++scan++_++ctrl++_++o` does
not assert.
. Restore `ap++_++debug=1`; re-open the DFD SIB and verify DFD access is

|`dfd++_++gate++_++chk` |DFD SIB forced closed and scan-ctrl inactive
while `ap++_++debug`=1

|`dfd++_++restore++_++chk` |DFD access resumes after `ap++_++debug`
clears
|===

*Pass Criteria*: DFD is reachable when ungated and forced closed when

scoreboard/reference-model errors.

===== 5.6 `dtp++_++3dcr++_++stap++_++sel++_++ds++_++test`

*Source Module*: `dtp++_++3dcr++_++stap++_++sel++_++ds++_++test`

*BFM / Model*: `ocah++_++jtag++_++vip`, OCAH-local STAP model

*Objective*: Downstream STAP selection

*Test Coverage*:

* TAP++_++3DCR (IR=0x0E) selects the downstream (I/O) STAP by writing
3DCR `stap++_++sel=1`, `tms++_++hold=1`, `config++_++hold=1`
* Configuration opens the DS SIB, writes the 3DCR, and closes the SIB in
one DR scan so subsequent scans carry only a 1-bit SIB per STAP
* Six data patterns (all-zeros, all-ones, alt-10, alt-01, walking-ones,
walking-zeros) transfer correctly to the DS PTAP slave model
* `feat++_++ctrl++_++i.sip++_++debug=0` gates the DS STAP; while gated
the DS PTAP model is not updated
* Recovery requires restoring `sip++_++debug=1`, JTAG reset (TRST) to
clear the stale `stap++_++sel`, and reconfiguration

*Test Procedure*:

[arabic]
. Load TAP++_++3DCR (IR=0x0E) into the PTAP via `OcahJtagTap`.
. Configure the DS STAP: open the DS SIB, write DS 3DCR
`stap++_++sel=1, tms++_++hold=1, config++_++hold=1`, then close the DS
SIB in the same DR scan; SEP and extra STAPs stay bypassed.
. Scan the extended IR: PTAP loaded with BYPASS, each STAP’s SIB bit
kept closed, DS downstream TAP IR included for the selected STAP.
. For each of the 6 data patterns, scan the extended DR (PTAP BYPASS 1
bit {plus} STAP SIB bits {plus} DS downstream TAP DR), read the DS PTAP
model, and verify the readback matches.
. Security gating:
[loweralpha]
.. Drive `feat++_++ctrl++_++i.sip++_++debug=0`, reconfigure the DS STAP,
and verify the DS PTAP model is unchanged (gated).
.. Restore required enables, assert JTAG reset (TRST) to clear the stale
gated `stap++_++sel=1` condition.
.. Reconfigure the DS STAP, re-scan extended IR/DR with new data, and
verify the DS PTAP model updates (access restored).

*Checkers*:

[width="100%",cols="52%,48%",options="header",]
|===
|Checker Type |Description
|`stap++_++ds++_++select++_++chk` |DS STAP TAP control active and DS SIB
closed after 3DCR configuration

|`stap++_++ds++_++data++_++chk` |All 6 data patterns match the DS PTAP
slave model readback

|`stap++_++ds++_++gate++_++chk` |DS PTAP model unchanged while
`sip++_++debug`=0

|`stap++_++ds++_++recover++_++chk` |DS access restored after restoring
`sip++_++debug` {plus} TRST {plus} reconfiguration
|===

*Pass Criteria*: The downstream STAP is selected and reachable with the
SIB closed for minimal chain length, all six patterns verify, and gating
plus recovery behave per `sip++_++debug`, with zero
scoreboard/reference-model errors.

'''''

===== 5.10 `dtp++_++ext++_++stap++_++scan++_++test`

*Source Module*: `dtp++_++ext++_++stap++_++scan++_++test`

*BFM / Model*: `ocah++_++jtag++_++vip`, OCAH-local STAP model

*Objective*: Extended STAP scan

*Test Coverage*:

* Extended STAP scan interface (`jtag++_++stap++_++host++_++scan++_*++`)
enabled via PTAP++_++3DCR.SELECT=1
* `jtag++_++stap++_++host++_++scan++_++ctrl++_++o` asserts scan control
during DR operations and data routes through
`jtag++_++stap++_++host++_++scan++_++out++_++o` /
`jtag++_++stap++_++host++_++scan++_++in++_++i`
* Clearing PTAP++_++3DCR.SELECT=0 deasserts the scan interface
* `stap++_++host++_++security++_++disable = !ap++_++debug` forces
`jtag++_++stap++_++host++_++scan++_++ctrl++_++o` to 0 and bypasses
scan-in (PTAP receives the `stap++_++host++_++scan++_++out` loopback
instead of external scan-in)
* Normal scan operation resumes after `ap++_++debug=1`

*Test Procedure*:

[arabic]
. Enable the extended STAP scan interface by writing
PTAP++_++3DCR.SELECT=1 via `OcahJtagTap`.
. Verify `jtag++_++stap++_++host++_++scan++_++ctrl++_++o` asserts during
DR operations; shift a known pattern out on
`jtag++_++stap++_++host++_++scan++_++out++_++o`.
. Verify the data returned on
`jtag++_++stap++_++host++_++scan++_++in++_++i` matches the expected
loopback/known response.
. Clear PTAP++_++3DCR.SELECT=0 and verify scan control deasserts.
. Security gating:
[loweralpha]
.. Re-enable scan (PTAP++_++3DCR.SELECT=1), then drive
`feat++_++ctrl++_++i.ap++_++debug=0`.
.. Verify `jtag++_++stap++_++host++_++scan++_++ctrl++_++o` is forced to
0 and scan-in is disconnected (PTAP sees the
`stap++_++host++_++scan++_++out` loopback rather than external scan-in).
.. Restore `ap++_++debug=1` and verify scan control and the data path
are restored.

*Checkers*:

[width="100%",cols="52%,48%",options="header",]
|===
|Checker Type |Description
|`ext++_++stap++_++enable++_++chk` |Scan control and data signals
function when PTAP++_++3DCR.SELECT=1

|`ext++_++stap++_++disable++_++chk` |Scan interface deactivates when
PTAP++_++3DCR.SELECT=0

|`ext++_++stap++_++gate++_++chk` |`ap++_++debug`=1 forces
`stap++_++host++_++scan++_++ctrl++_++o` to 0 and bypasses scan-in

|`ext++_++stap++_++restore++_++chk` |Normal STAP host scan resumes after
`ap++_++debug` clears
|===

*Pass Criteria*: The extended STAP scan interface operates when
selected, deactivates when deselected, is suppressed and
loopback-bypassed when `ap++_++debug=0`, and recovers when restored,
with zero scoreboard/reference-model errors.

'''''

===== 5.11 `dtp++_++3dcr++_++config++_++hold++_++test`

*Source Module*: `dtp++_++3dcr++_++config++_++hold++_++test`

*BFM / Model*: `ocah++_++jtag++_++vip`, OCAH-local STAP model

*Objective*: 3DCR config hold

*Test Coverage*:

* CONFIG++_++HOLD in both PTAP++_++3DCR and STAP 3DCR protects register
contents across Test-Logic-Reset (TLR)
* With CONFIG++_++HOLD=1, SELECT (PTAP) and SELECT/TMS++_++HOLD (STAP)
survive a forced TLR
* With CONFIG++_++HOLD=0, a TLR clears the affected 3DCR fields to 0
* Configuration values are exercised through `OcahJtagTap` IR/DR scans
against the STAP reference model

*Test Procedure*:

[arabic]
. Write PTAP++_++3DCR with CONFIG++_++HOLD=1, SELECT=1; Update-DR.
. Write the STAP 3DCR with CONFIG++_++HOLD=1, SELECT=1, TMS++_++HOLD=1;
Update-DR.
. Force TLR (5x TMS=1).
[loweralpha]
.. Read PTAP++_++3DCR and verify SELECT=1 preserved.
.. Read the STAP 3DCR and verify SELECT=1 and TMS++_++HOLD=1 preserved.
. Clear CONFIG++_++HOLD=0 in both PTAP and STAP 3DCRs; force TLR again.
[loweralpha]
.. Read both 3DCRs and verify all bits cleared to 0.

*Checkers*:

[width="100%",cols="52%,48%",options="header",]
|===
|Checker Type |Description
|`config++_++hold++_++preserve++_++chk` |CONFIG++_++HOLD=1 preserves
PTAP SELECT and STAP SELECT/TMS++_++HOLD across TLR

|`config++_++hold++_++clear++_++chk` |CONFIG++_++HOLD=0 lets TLR clear
the 3DCR fields to 0

|`config++_++hold++_++data++_++chk` |3DCR readback through `OcahJtagTap`
matches the STAP reference model
|===

*Pass Criteria*: CONFIG++_++HOLD=1 holds both 3DCRs across TLR while
CONFIG++_++HOLD=0 allows TLR to clear them, matching the reference
model, with zero scoreboard/reference-model errors.

'''''

===== 5.12 `dtp++_++3dcr++_++tms++_++hold++_++test`

*Source Module*: `dtp++_++3dcr++_++tms++_++hold++_++test`

*BFM / Model*: `ocah++_++jtag++_++vip`, OCAH-local STAP model

*Objective*: 3DCR TMS hold

*Test Coverage*:

to a deselected STAP
* TMS++_++HOLD=1 parks the deselected STAP TMS high (drives toward TLR);
TMS++_++HOLD=0 parks it low (drives toward RTI)
* Parked value is observed on the STAP TAP control output while the STAP
is deselected (SELECT=0)

scans

*Test Procedure*:

[arabic]
. Select a STAP via the 3DCR (SELECT=1, TMS++_++HOLD=1); Update-DR.
. Deselect the STAP (SELECT=0, TMS++_++HOLD=1); Update-DR.
[loweralpha]
.. Verify the STAP TAP control output drives TMS=1 (parked high toward
TLR).
. Repeat with TMS++_++HOLD=0:
[loweralpha]
.. Select then deselect the STAP with TMS++_++HOLD=0; Update-DR.
.. Verify the STAP TAP control output drives TMS=0 (parked low toward
RTI).
. Confirm the parked TMS tracks the configured TMS++_++HOLD against the
STAP reference model.

*Checkers*:

[width="100%",cols="52%,48%",options="header",]
|===
|Checker Type |Description
|`tms++_++hold++_++high++_++chk` |Deselected STAP TMS parks high (TLR)
when TMS++_++HOLD=1

|`tms++_++hold++_++low++_++chk` |Deselected STAP TMS parks low (RTI)
when TMS++_++HOLD=0

|`tms++_++hold++_++data++_++chk` |Parked TMS value matches the STAP
reference model for each polarity
|===

*Pass Criteria*: A deselected STAP parks its TMS at the configured
TMS++_++HOLD value for both polarities, matching the reference model,
with zero scoreboard/reference-model errors.

'''''

==== 6. XTRIG, CTP, and CTM Scenarios

===== 6.1 `dtp++_++xtrig++_++wire++_++or++_++test`

*Source Module*: `dtp++_++xtrig++_++wire++_++or++_++test`

*BFM / Model*: `ocah++_++axi++_++vip`, OCAH-local CTP/CTM BFMs

*Objective*: CTP wire-OR

*Test Coverage*:

* CTP wire-OR mode (CONFIG.MODE=0) pulse synchronization with
configurable pulse stretching
* Output pulse width equals (STRETCH++_++MULT{plus}1) clock cycles for
both a wide multiplier and the single-cycle minimum
* External CT++_++Req++_++in input synchronized into the `clk++_++i`
domain before delivery
* STATUS.BUSY accurately reflects pulse activity (asserts during the
stretched pulse, clears after)
* Internal cross-trigger pulse delivered to the CTM destination on an
external trigger

*Test Procedure*:

[arabic]
. Configure CTP++[++0++]++ via `OcahAxiLiteMaster`: CONFIG.MODE=0
(Wire-OR), CONFIG.INVERT=0, STRETCH++_++MULT=0x000F (16 clocks).
. Drive a single-cycle internal cross-trigger request to CTP++[++0++]++
(via CTM routing).
[loweralpha]
.. Observe CT++_++Req++_++out on the CTP BFM; verify it asserts and
remains asserted for exactly (STRETCH++_++MULT{plus}1) clock cycles.
.. Read CTP++[++0++]++ STATUS; verify BUSY=1 while the stretched pulse
is active and BUSY=0 after it completes.
. Drive a short CT++_++Req++_++in pulse from the CTP BFM (simulate an
external trigger).
[loweralpha]
.. Verify the input is synchronized to `clk++_++i` and the resulting
internal cross-trigger pulse is delivered to the programmed CTM
destination.
. Reprogram STRETCH++_++MULT=0x0000 (1 clock), repeat the internal
trigger, and verify a single-cycle output pulse with the same BUSY
behavior.

*Checkers*:

[width="100%",cols="52%,48%",options="header",]
|===
|Checker Type |Description
|`ctp++_++wire++_++or++_++csr++_++chk` |CONFIG/STRETCH++_++MULT writes
read back the programmed route configuration

|`ctp++_++wire++_++or++_++stretch++_++chk` |Output pulse width equals
(STRETCH++_++MULT{plus}1) clocks for both multiplier values

|`ctp++_++wire++_++or++_++sync++_++chk` |External CT++_++Req++_++in is
synchronized to `clk++_++i` and delivered to the CTM destination

|`ctp++_++wire++_++or++_++busy++_++chk` |STATUS.BUSY asserts during the
stretched pulse and clears afterward
|===

*Pass Criteria*: Wire-OR pulse stretching, input synchronization, and
BUSY tracking match the reference model for both stretch settings, with
zero scoreboard/reference-model errors.

'''''

===== 6.2 `dtp++_++xtrig++_++p2p++_++test`

*Source Module*: `dtp++_++xtrig++_++p2p++_++test`

*BFM / Model*: `ocah++_++axi++_++vip`, OCAH-local CTP/CTM BFMs

*Objective*: CTP point-to-point

*Test Coverage*:

directions
* Request path FSM sequences
REQ++_++PHASE1-++>++REQ++_++PHASE2-++>++IDLE; response path sequences
RESP++_++PHASE1-++>++RESP++_++PHASE2-++>++IDLE
* STATUS register reflects live handshake state and STATUS.BUSY tracks
the in-flight handshake
* Internal cross-trigger delivered to the CTM destination on an external
trigger

[arabic]
. Configure CTP++[++0++]++ via `OcahAxiLiteMaster`: CONFIG.MODE=1
(Point-to-Point), CONFIG.INVERT=0.

routing).
[loweralpha]

.. Drive CT++_++Ack++_++in from the CTP BFM (simulate the external
responder).
.. Verify the handshake completes: CT++_++Req++_++out deasserts,
CT++_++Ack++_++in deasserts, FSM returns to IDLE.
. Read CTP++[++0++]++ STATUS; verify BUSY=0 after completion.
. Drive CT++_++Req++_++in from the CTP BFM (simulate an external
trigger).
[loweralpha]
.. Verify CT++_++Ack++_++out asserts and the response-side handshake
completes through RESP++_++PHASE1-++>++RESP++_++PHASE2-++>++IDLE.
.. Verify the internal cross-trigger is delivered to the programmed CTM
destination.

*Checkers*:

[width="100%",cols="52%,48%",options="header",]
|===
|Checker Type |Description
|`ctp++_++p2p++_++handshake++_++chk` |Req/Ack 4-phase handshake
completes in both directions

|`ctp++_++p2p++_++fsm++_++chk` |FSM sequences through REQ++_++PHASE1/2
and RESP++_++PHASE1/2 back to IDLE

|`ctp++_++p2p++_++busy++_++chk` |STATUS.BUSY asserts during and clears
after each handshake

|`ctp++_++p2p++_++deliver++_++chk` |External trigger is delivered to the
CTM destination
|===

*Pass Criteria*: Both handshake directions complete with correct FSM
sequencing, BUSY behavior, and trigger delivery, with zero
scoreboard/reference-model errors.

===== 6.3 `dtp++_++xtrig++_++reset++_++test`

*Source Module*: `dtp++_++xtrig++_++reset++_++test`

*BFM / Model*: `ocah++_++axi++_++vip`, OCAH-local CTP/CTM BFMs

*Objective*: CTP reset recovery

*Test Coverage*:

* CONFIG.RESET recovery from a P2P handshake deadlock (stalled external
ack)
* System reset (`rst++_++n++_++i`) clears all CTP register state back to
defaults
* No spurious GPIO/trigger activity asserted during either reset path
* Normal operation resumes after recovery (a fresh handshake proceeds)

*Test Procedure*:

[arabic]
. Configure CTP++[++0++]++ in P2P mode and initiate a handshake.
[loweralpha]
.. Stall the external CT++_++Ack++_++in on the CTP BFM to simulate a
deadlock.
.. Read STATUS; verify BUSY=1 (handshake stuck).
. Recover via CONFIG.RESET.
[loweralpha]
.. Write CONFIG.RESET=1 via `OcahAxiLiteMaster`.
.. Verify the handshake is force-cleared: STATUS.BUSY=0 and
CT++_++Req++_++out deasserts.
.. Write CONFIG.RESET=0 to release; verify CTP returns to normal
operation and a new handshake can proceed to completion.
. Assert system reset (`rst++_++n++_++i`).
[loweralpha]
.. Verify all CTP registers return to reset/default values on readback
after release.
.. Verify no spurious GPIO toggles occur during the reset window.
. Reprogram a post-reset route and verify it operates correctly.

*Checkers*:

[width="100%",cols="52%,48%",options="header",]
|===
|Checker Type |Description
|`ctp++_++config++_++reset++_++chk` |CONFIG.RESET clears a stalled P2P
handshake (BUSY=0, CT++_++Req++_++out deasserts)

|`ctp++_++sys++_++reset++_++chk` |System reset returns all CTP CSRs to
default values

|`ctp++_++reset++_++no++_++spurious++_++chk` |No spurious GPIO/trigger
activity during either reset

|`ctp++_++reset++_++recovery++_++chk` |CTP resumes normal operation and
a post-reset handshake completes
|===

*Pass Criteria*: CONFIG.RESET recovers from deadlock, system reset
restores defaults with no spurious activity, and post-reset operation
matches the reference model, with zero scoreboard/reference-model
errors.

'''''

===== 6.5 `dtp++_++xtrig++_++rand++_++deterministic++_++csr++_++sweep++_++test`

conditions.

programmed value and that reserved bits behave as specified.

|===

preserved across skewed accesses

===== 6.10 `dtp++_++xtrig++_++axi++_++channel++_++skew++_++read++_++decode++_++backpressure++_++test`

*Source Module*: `dtp++_++xtrig++_++axi++_++channel++_++skew++_++test`

*BFM / Model*: `ocah++_++axi++_++vip`, OCAH-local XTRIG monitor

*Objective*: Read decode backpressure

*Test Coverage*:

* Unmapped XTRIG reads routed to the xbar default DECERR slave
(`axi++_++lite++_++xbar.default++_++ar++_++mst++_++port++[_++en++]++`)
* RREADY held low so a second ARVALID must stay stable while ARREADY is
low (`axi++_++lite++_++demux.ar++_++valid++_++stable` / `ar++_++stable`)
* Both unmapped reads return DECERR; no deadlock on the backpressured
read path
* Stable ARADDR and read data through the decode-backpressure window

*Test Procedure*:

[arabic]
. Idle all manual AXI-Lite master outputs after reset; confirm the XTRIG
xbar address map has no valid slave at `0x02B0` and above.
. Issue an unmapped read to `0x02B0` and hold the DECERR response by
keeping the master RREADY=0.
. While the first response is held, issue a second unmapped read to
`0x02F0`.
[loweralpha]
.. Verify ARVALID remains asserted and ARADDR remains stable while
ARREADY is low (read-stability assertions hit).
. Release RREADY, allow the second AR to complete, and verify the second
response is also DECERR.
. Confirm via the XTRIG monitor that both reads returned DECERR with no
deadlock and stable channel behavior throughout.

*Checkers*:

[width="100%",cols="52%,48%",options="header",]
|===
|Checker Type |Description
|`xtrig++_++read++_++decerr++_++chk` |Both unmapped reads return DECERR
from the xbar default slave

|`xtrig++_++read++_++ar++_++stable++_++chk` |ARVALID/ARADDR remain
stable while ARREADY is low

|`xtrig++_++read++_++default++_++ar++_++chk` |The xbar
`default++_++ar++_++mst++_++port++[_++en++]++` read path receives real
success hits

|`xtrig++_++read++_++no++_++deadlock++_++chk` |The backpressured read
path completes without deadlock
|===

*Pass Criteria*: Both unmapped reads return DECERR with stable channels
through the backpressure window and the xbar default-read and demux
read-stability assertions covered, with zero scoreboard/reference-model

registers to `0x0000++_++0000`

the reset

. Establish pre-reset wire-OR state:
[loweralpha]

. Assert system reset (`rst++_++n++_++i`) while wire-OR routes are
active, then wait for reset deassertion.
. Verify clearing and quiescence:
[loweralpha]
.. Read all `CT++_++SRC++[++N++]_++CONFIG++_++0` and confirm each is
`0x0000++_++0000`.
.. Confirm the OCAH-local CTM/CTP monitors observe no spurious trigger
on any destination during or after reset.
. Reconfigure a fresh wire-OR route post-reset, pulse the source, and
verify correct multicast routing resumes.

*Checkers*:

[width="100%",cols="52%,48%",options="header",]
|===
|Checker Type |Description
|`ctm++_++reset++_++wire++_++or++_++mode++_++csr++_++chk` |All
`CT++_++SRC++[++N++]_++CONFIG++_++0` read back `0x0000++_++0000` after
reset

|`ctm++_++reset++_++wire++_++or++_++mode++_++nospur++_++chk` |No
spurious trigger on any destination during or after reset

|`ctm++_++reset++_++wire++_++or++_++mode++_++route++_++chk` |Post-reset
wire-OR reconfiguration routes correctly against the reference model
|===

*Pass Criteria*: Reset clears all wire-OR routing state with no spurious
destination toggles, and post-reset multicast routing matches the
reference model, with zero scoreboard/reference-model errors.

'''''

*Pass Criteria*: Reset returns all routing state to defaults for both
modes with no spurious activity, and both wire-OR and P2P routes
reprogram correctly, with zero scoreboard/reference-model errors.

'''''

===== 6.22 `dtp++_++ctm++_++rand++_++all++_++scenarios++_++test`

*Source Module*: `dtp++_++ctm++_++rand++_++test`

*BFM / Model*: `ocah++_++axi++_++vip`, OCAH-local CTM/CTP BFMs

*Objective*: Random CTM all scenarios

*Test Coverage*:

* Randomized `CT++_++DST++_++SELECT` masks across all CTM sources
spanning both wire-OR and P2P routing
* Random source/destination pairs and trigger timing across CLA and CTP
classes
* All observed routing matches the CTM reference model for every random
configuration
* No routing corruption or state leakage between successive random
configurations
* Wire-OR superposition correct whenever random masks overlap on a
destination

*Test Procedure*:

[arabic]
. For N iterations (e.g. 20):
[loweralpha]
.. Generate random `CT++_++DST++_++SELECT` values for all CTM sources,
mixing single-bit and multi-bit masks across CLA and CTP destinations.
.. Write all `CT++_++SRC++[++N++]_++CONFIG++_++0` registers via
`OcahAxiLiteMaster` and read each back to confirm the programmed mask.
.. Drive random cross-trigger requests on random source ports via the
OCAH-local CTM/CTP BFMs with randomized timing.
.. Verify each request appears at exactly its configured destinations
and that overlapping destinations OR correctly.
.. Confirm no residual routing carries over from the previous
iteration’s configuration.

*Checkers*:

[width="100%",cols="52%,48%",options="header",]
|===
|Checker Type |Description
|`ctm++_++rand++_++all++_++scenarios++_++csr++_++chk` |Every randomized
`CT++_++DST++_++SELECT` reads back as programmed

|`ctm++_++rand++_++all++_++scenarios++_++route++_++chk` |All observed
routing matches the reference model across iterations

|`ctm++_++rand++_++all++_++scenarios++_++or++_++chk` |Overlapping random
masks OR correctly at shared destinations

|`ctm++_++rand++_++all++_++scenarios++_++noleak++_++chk` |No routing
state leaks between successive configurations
|===

*Pass Criteria*: Every random mixed-mode configuration routes exactly as
modeled with correct wire-OR superposition and no inter-configuration
leakage, with zero scoreboard/reference-model errors.

'''''

===== 6.23 `dtp++_++ctm++_++rand++_++wire++_++or++_++only++_++test`

and the routing mask is honored.

contributing sources; verify non-selected destinations stay idle.

===== 6.27 `dtp++_++ctm++_++rand++_++deterministic++_++csr++_++sweep++_++test`

*Source Module*: `dtp++_++ctm++_++rand++_++test`

*BFM / Model*: `ocah++_++axi++_++vip`, OCAH-local CTM BFM

*Objective*: CTM CSR deterministic sweep

*Test Coverage*:

* Deterministic closure of CTM generated-regblock byte-enable and
masked-write condition holes that random selection can miss
* Full-word, per-byte, and masked writes to
`CT++_++SRC++[++N++]_++CONFIG++_++0.CT++_++DST++_++SELECT` with readback
after each
* Reserved bits read back as zero and are unaffected by writes
* Internal route decode tracks each programmed `CT++_++DST++_++SELECT`
value

*Test Procedure*:

[arabic]
. Bring the XTRIG CSR path to idle and disable all CTM source routing.
. Sweep the CTM routing CSR fields deterministically via
`OcahAxiLiteMaster`:
[loweralpha]
.. Apply full-word writes, then per-byte-enable writes, then masked
writes to `CT++_++SRC++[++N++]_++CONFIG++_++0.CT++_++DST++_++SELECT` to
exercise each generated byte-enable and masked-write condition.
.. Read back after each write and compare the defined destination-select
field against the expected value.
.. Confirm reserved bits remain zero and are not disturbed by adjacent
writes.
. For representative swept values, confirm the internal route decode
matches the programmed `CT++_++DST++_++SELECT` via the OCAH-local CTM
monitor.
. Disable all CTM source routing before test exit.

*Checkers*:

[width="100%",cols="52%,48%",options="header",]
|===
|Checker Type |Description
|`ctm++_++rand++_++deterministic++_++csr++_++sweep++_++csr++_++chk`
|Full-word, byte-enable, and masked-write readbacks match expected
`CT++_++DST++_++SELECT` values

|`ctm++_++rand++_++deterministic++_++csr++_++sweep++_++rsvd++_++chk`
|Reserved bits read back zero and are undisturbed

|`ctm++_++rand++_++deterministic++_++csr++_++sweep++_++route++_++chk`
|Internal route decode tracks the programmed destination mask against
the reference model
|===

*Pass Criteria*: Every byte-enable and masked-write condition in the CTM
regblock is exercised and reads back correctly with reserved bits clear
and route decode matching, with zero scoreboard/reference-model errors.

'''''

===== 6.28 `dtp++_++ctm++_++rand++_++all++_++source++_++select++_++coverage++_++test`

*Source Module*: `dtp++_++ctm++_++rand++_++test`

*BFM / Model*: `ocah++_++axi++_++vip`, OCAH-local CTM BFM

*Objective*: CTM all-source select coverage

*Test Coverage*:

* Deterministic closure of per-source select mapping for every defined
CTM source-select CSR (`CT++_++SRC1++_++CONFIG++_++0` through
`CT++_++SRC25++_++CONFIG++_++0`)
* Single-destination, paired-destination, and inverted-destination
`CT++_++DST++_++SELECT` masks written and read back at every source
index
* No source index aliases another CTM source register
* Direct exercise of all generated CTM source-select mapping code in the
default DTP configuration

*Test Procedure*:

[arabic]
. Assert power-on reset, bring the XTRIG CSR path to idle, and disable
all CTM source routing.
. For every defined CTM source index, via `OcahAxiLiteMaster`:
[loweralpha]
.. Write a single-destination `CT++_++DST++_++SELECT` mask, read back,
and compare the defined destination-select field.
.. Write a paired-destination mask, read back, and compare.
.. Write an inverted-destination mask, read back, and compare.
.. Confirm the written value affects only this source register and no
neighboring `CT++_++SRC++[++N++]_++CONFIG++_++0` (no aliasing).
. Sample per-source select coverage so every
`CT++_++SRC1..25++_++CONFIG++_++0` mapping is touched.
. Disable all CTM source routing again before test exit.

*Checkers*:

[width="100%",cols="52%,48%",options="header",]
|===
|Checker Type |Description
|`ctm++_++rand++_++all++_++source++_++select++_++coverage++_++csr++_++chk`
|Every defined source-select CSR accepts and returns the
single/paired/inverted mask

|`ctm++_++rand++_++all++_++source++_++select++_++coverage++_++alias++_++chk`
|No source index aliases another CTM source register

|`ctm++_++rand++_++all++_++source++_++select++_++coverage++_++route++_++chk`
|Per-source route decode matches the programmed mask against the
reference model
|===

*Pass Criteria*: Every defined CTM source-select CSR is touched with
single, paired, and inverted masks and reads back correctly with no
aliasing across source indices, with zero scoreboard/reference-model
errors.

'''''

=== Known Limitations

[width="100%",cols="34%,21%,45%",options="header",]
|===
|Limitation |Impact |Resolution Path
|CTP/CTM generated CSR blocks are stubbed for Verilator |XTRIG CSR
scenarios require non-stub CSR behavior |Replace stubs with
Verilator-compatible CSR RTL or run these tests on a commercial
simulator

|iJTAG and OCH cross-trigger protocols do not have established public
cocotb VIP |No public protocol VIP identified |Implement OCAH-local DTP
BFMs in `env/`

|Lifecycle is commonly held in all-one/full-debug mode for base bring-up
|Security-gating scenarios require explicit lifecycle stimulus |Add
directed lifecycle scenarios for security-gating coverage

|SV covergroups are not supported by Verilator |Functional coverage must
be Python-side or commercial-sim |See `DTP++_++FCOV.md`
|===
